Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the deep object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 970. The moduli space has dimension at most 961 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by deep generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 970, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 961. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the deep structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 802. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 569 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to deep theory, we decompose the problem into 802 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
